## Type Chart

| Type | Strong Against | Weak Against |
|------|----------------|--------------|
| Steel | Fairy, Rock, Ice | Fire, Water, Electric |
| Electric | Water, Flying | Ground, Electric, Grass |
| Psychic | Fighting, Poison | Dark, Steel |
| Dark | Psychic, Ghost | Fighting, Fairy |
| Dragon | Dragon | Steel, Fairy |
| Fire | Grass, Steel, Ice | Water, Rock |
| Water | Fire, Ground, Rock | Grass, Electric |
| Ground | Fire, Electric, Steel | Grass, Flying |
| Rock | Fire, Ice, Flying | Fighting, Ground, Steel |
| Grass | Water, Ground, Rock | Fire, Poison, Flying |
| Fighting | Normal, Steel, Dark | Flying, Psychic, Fairy |
| Normal | -- | Rock, Steel, Ghost (immune) |
| Fairy | Fighting, Dragon, Dark | Fire, Steel, Poison |
| Ghost | Psychic, Ghost | Dark |
| Ice | Dragon, Grass, Ground | Fire, Water, Steel |
| Flying | Fighting, Grass | Electric, Rock, Steel |

**Effectiveness:** 2.0 = super effective, 0.5 = not very, 0.0 = immune.
